Model before rewriting:

find m: matrix indexed by [int(-2, 3)] of record {flag: bool, value: int(-1, 2)}
find i: int(-2, 3..4)
find caught: bool
find exceptional: bool
find masked: bool
find selected: bool

such that

(selected) <-> (m[i] = record {value = -1,flag = false}),
(masked) <-> (or([i = 4,m[i] = record {value = -1,flag = false};int(1..)])),
(caught) <-> (catchUndef(m[i], record {value = -1,flag = false}) = record {value = -1,flag = false}),
(exceptional) <-> (allDifferentExcept([record {value = -1,flag = false},record {value = -1,flag = false};int(1..2)], m[i]))
